## Practice
- **Practice's places name themselves alone (owner, 2026-11-26: "remove all the thigns that i didnt asked for, make more clean interface"):** the overview's Theory rows said what each page holds ("Chord types, voicings, inversions", "Scales, fingerings, key signatures") under pictures that already show it, and the Tools carried a description, their own settings ("120 BPM · 4/4", "A4 = 440 Hz", the tool's page holds them) or a history the Chord finder no longer keeps ("Last: Cmaj7"); all went. The Exercises keep their counts, the size of each collection.
- **Clean: nothing the owner did not ask for (owner, 2026-11-26: "remove all the thigns that i didnt asked for, make more clean interface"):** each board keeps what a ruling asked for and what the page cannot work without; a feature drawn on our own initiative, or a second home for what another page does, goes. First: the Chords page's run of chords (the staff's +, its empty bar, the name over the staff), the Chord player's job.
- **Songs in the Chord player (owner, 2026-11-21: "add this chordsheet to the chords player", Chordify's PDF of David Preuteasa Band's Isus s-a născut):** the Chord player's library (Chord sheets, from the sheet's name › All chord sheets) holds Songs between My chord sheets and Progressions, each a row of its name and who it is by; a tap puts the song in hand with its tempo, Undo in the toast, as a progression opens. Isus s-a născut is its first, F major at 130, bar for bar from the chart: A♯ and D♯ written B♭ and E♭ as the key spells them, the three-beat opening a whole bar of F (a sheet has no pickup), three Verses in F and the Chorus in G from bar 71, the closing rests left out. The songs are `entities/chord-sheet/content/songs.ts`.
- **One setting, one order and one word (loop, the Practice review, 2026-11-20):** Written as read Numerals · Nashville · Chords on a progression and Chords · Numerals · Nashville in the Chord player; Voicing read Voice-led · Block on a progression and Smooth voice leading · Block chords in the Chord player. One setting is one shape everywhere: Written as is Chords · Numerals · Nashville (the chord first, as a musician reads a chart; `PROGRESSION_NAMES` the one list, the Chord player's own list gone), Voicing is Voice-led · Block, on the boards and in the app.
- **A library's folded section stays folded (loop, the Practice review, 2026-11-20):** a shelf folded in Finger technique, Jazz vocabulary, Accompaniment or Progressions opened again on the next visit, as each fold lived in its page's memory alone, where every other Practice page keeps its folds in Settings (`useFold`). It keeps them there too, one place per library and section (`library:<kind>:<section>`, `libraryFoldId`), shared by the phone's runs, the laptop's table and Edit, so a shelf folded on one size is folded on the other. Checked at 402 and 1710: folded, left for Practice, back, still folded.
- **Progressions filter by key, as their board (loop, the Practice review, 2026-11-20):** the Progressions board's Sort and filter holds Key (Major key 45 · Minor key 25, each with its count); the app had no Key filter. It has it now, in the URL (`mode`), with its chip and in Clear filters; under Minor key a progression written in both keys shows its minor line and says Minor. Every filter choice in a practice library carries its count as the board draws it (Level's too, in Accompaniment and the Exercises), the count at 60% after the name. The board's sorts stay As arranged and Name: Recently used is a place, not a sort.
- **A pattern's Publish is a window over the pattern (loop, the Practice review, 2026-11-20):** it was the last thing's Publish with an address of its own (`/practice/accompaniment/patterns/$patternRef/publish`), against the ruling that no thing's Settings or Publish has one. Its ··· › Publish… now opens it over the pattern as a song's and a progression's do (`SettingsWindow`: on a laptop the 720 window centred, on a phone a tall sheet with Done), held in the history entry, so Back closes it; the route and its page are gone. Its head said "Your song" under a pattern; it says "Your pattern". Canvas: Pattern, PatternLaptop (THINGWIN).

- **Learn's laptop board in the place frame, and a level row's words at their normal height (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-20):** the Learn laptop board drew its title in the 56 sub-page bar (`LTITLEBAR`), where Practice's board and the app give a place's own page the 88 bar, so its name stood 16 high and everything under it 8; the kit gains `LPLACEBAR`, the place's 88 bar, used by Learn's boards. In the app a level row's name and line took 24 and 20 of line height where the board's take their normal 20 and 18, 2 apart; now as the board, measured within 1 at 402 and 1710.

- **Practice's overview where its boards set it (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-20):** the first heading stood 2 high on a phone and 15 high on a laptop; a place row's tile sat 8 in where the board's `PROW` sets it 12 in, so its words started 4 left; a laptop card was 16 round with 14 of padding and 10 between its picture and name, its name at the body's line height, the cards 12 apart, where the board's `PCARD` is 14 round, 16 in, 12 under the picture, its name and line at normal height, 16 apart. Each is the board's now, measured within 1 at 402 and 1710. The board's Jazz vocabulary said 4 exercises where the app holds 10 (Barry Harris 5, Piano With Jonny 5); it says 10.

- **One heart, Lucide's, on every board; a library row's line cut at the letter (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-20):** the boards drew the heart as their own path (a narrower, pointed shape) in six generators, where the app wears Lucide's heart, so a row's ♡ read smaller on the canvas; every board's heart is Lucide's now, with its round caps (the libraries, Songs, a song's page, Explore, a folder, Settings' swipe acts, a pattern's page). In the app a library row's line was clamped to one line, which cuts at a word ("plays the…"), where the boards cut it at the letter with an ellipsis ("plays the ch…"), at the line's normal 18 two under the name; the row's face takes the boards' cut and height (`ROW_LIBRARY_FACE`).

- **A Practice library's search 8 under its large bar, as Songs' (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-20):** the Progressions, Accompaniment, Finger technique and Jazz vocabulary boards stood their search 4 under the large title's bar (122) and the place chips 16 under it, where the Songs board and the app stand the search 8 under it (126, the rule's 8 to 16) with the chips 12 under; the boards (`r41_lib.py`) now do the same, the chips where they were. The small bar's pages (All songs) keep their 4, board and app alike. The other tools paired again and matching: the Chord player's first sheet, the Tuner's No microphone, the Chord finder's Nothing played, phone and laptop.

- **The Chord player's laptop keys C2 to C7, its add-a-chord place one column, a chord's raised part at the boards' size (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-20):** the laptop board drew the Chord player's keys F2 to A4 stretched across 1462, where every laptop keyboard on the canvas runs C2 to C7; both the board and the app now run C2 to C7 on a laptop (`CHORD_PLAYER_LAPTOP_KEYS`), the phone's F2 to A4 kept, each C named on both (the kit's key faces read a list ending in L as the laptop's). The + that adds a chord took two of a laptop's sixteen columns (107) where the board's takes one (50.5); on a phone the bars stood 4 apart where the board's stand 8, so the + was 89.5, now 86.5 as drawn. A chord symbol's raised part (the 7 of G7, the ° of B°) was 0.58 of the name, 11.6 on a 20 name, where the boards' `sup` is max(12px, .6em); `LeadSymbol` takes the boards' size. The board's key tiles raised only a number (B° stood on the line, the app raised it); they raise what follows the root and its m, as the app and the chart's own boxes, never the m of Dm.

- **The Circle of fifths' board opens on C major, as the app (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-20):** the board opened on G major (1 sharp) where the app opens on C major (No sharps or flats), so the two could not be measured on one key; the board's default key is C now, as Chords' Cmaj7 and Scales' C major were set to the app's.

- **Every board's noteheads are Bravura's (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-20):** after the Practice staves, the song page's and editor's Amazing Grace, Hanon's figure, a lesson's staff block and a take's written bar still drew their heads as rotated ellipses (a half note a stroked ring), where the engine engraves Bravura's black and half heads. The kit's one `HEADG` (`calm_parts.py`) draws Bravura's glyph at the staff's space, centred on its note, in the note's colour (the selected honey, a played note soft); `bravura_glyphs.py` gains the half head (E0A3) from the font. Song, Editor, EditorDraft, Lesson and Takes, both sizes.

- **The Chord player asks for no gesture alone (laws, A phone is not a small laptop 6 and Cognitive biases 1,
  2026-11-18):** a chord's sheet opened only on a double tap inside 400ms, and on a laptop the key's chords went into a
  bar only by dragging, with "Drag one into a bar." under them as an explanation line. A tap selects a chord and a tap
  on the chord already chosen opens its sheet (Numbers' and Keynote's tap, then tap again to edit), at any pace; each
  of the key's chords is a button that puts it in the chosen chord's place, dragging kept as the shortcut, its tooltip
  "Tap or drag into a bar", and the line under them is gone. A bar's length keeps its drag handle and its twin, the
  sheet's Duration.
- **The Chord player's layout is the learner's (2026-11-18, "more user customizable ui like draggable resizable, hidible, minimizable toolbars … genos controls on phone … occupies too much space … saved and resetable in the app setitngs … opens the last opened … settinsg should be refactored"; canvas R76):** Keys, Controls, Genos and Chords are panels placed, sized, minimized and hidden in Edit layout (drag, or tap and tap a place: WCAG 2.5.7), saved per page, reset from the gear or Settings › General › Page layouts with Undo; the Genos Compact by default on a phone and minimizable from its head; the sheet's name a menu of recent sheets, opening on the last; settings regrouped Sound · Playing · Chart · MIDI · Layout; ··· a menu, not a sheet; the Follow switch drew off while on (its colour keys were missing), fixed. Spec: `practice-chord-player.md`, first ruling.

  turns the thing on or off (Apple HIG, NN/g); the chosen radio card holds its own switch.
- **Text never touches its control's edge (R42, canvas 85):** "these piano table options still have padding
  issues. use proper skills": the layout check fails any label under 6px from its control's edge; a row whose
  segments do not fit beside its label puts the label above.
- **A setting works in every view, and a tappable thing looks tappable (R42, canvas 84):** "I dont like the labels
  settings and that they are only available in 2d": Labels (Note names · Degrees · None) now draw on the 3D piano
  too;
  "make the chord that it is editable more clear": the name's parts are filled pills with chevrons.
- **Look at it before it is sent (R42, v82):** "can you look at the websites before you send them … paddings and
  voicing button placed weirdly": a bar's button ran off the screen's edge because only the changed part was
  screenshotted. Every publish: the layout check in every changed state, then the eye on the whole screen, the
  floating bar and the edges, phone and laptop. A floating group is symmetric around its main button.
- **A button is named for what it opens, not its value (R42, v81):** "on the close voicing it looks like a close
  button": the bar's Voicing button showed "Close". Check every value a control can show for a word that reads as a
  command (Close, Open, Off, Back, Clear).
- **Ask, then draw the choices side by side (R42, v80):** after 25 rounds of guesses on Chords, three rounds of
  numbered questions (a recommendation with each, "yes" accepts) settled the page's job, the 3D piano, key colours,
  fingering, hands and settings homes in minutes; the look, which talk cannot settle, went up as two working designs
  to pick from. Research is capped at about 50 Firecrawl credits a round.
- **A canvas feature is proven where the owner sees it (R42, v79):** "the 3d piano doenst work": it ran in the local
  runtime and failed in the published page. Test a board under a policy that allows no connection (CSP
  `connect-src 'none'`); inline assets are parsed, never fetched.
- **Hands is a menu button, not a row (R42, v79):** "I dont like the hand controls and settings": no segmented row
  or cards on the page; one button by Play opens Both · Right · Left (flowkey). A choice about sharing notes between
  hands is a voicing.
- **Use the app's real thing, never a redrawing of it (R42, v78):** "DONT you see how awful this looks … proper
  implementations like the 3d piano does": six rounds of drawn hands were rejected; the Player's 3D hands, run
  live in the canvas, were the answer. When the app already has the component, the canvas runs it (bundle it with
  Vite into the board), and its settings reuse the component's own control (Left · Both · Right).
- **Hands belong at the keys (R42, v77):** "I dont like it, be creative and think outside of the box", of a hand
  diagram beside the staff, however correct its numerals: the hands lie under the keyboard and each finger reaches
  its key (flowkey, the courses app). When a correct drawing is still rejected, change where the thing lives, not
  its styling.
- **Research until the convention is known, then draw it (R42, v76):** "make proper implementation of the finger
  numbers colors and research more", "you didn't research enough": one saved page was not research. Finger numbers
  are upright bold numerals in fingertip circles (Hoffman, Faber, Pianote), never italic, never floating badges.
  Before drawing a teaching diagram, view how at least three teaching sources draw it and cite them.
- **Research the owner's material first, then one decision (R42, v74):** "research more first and use the piano
  data and its transcriptions and examples": the staff ties the notes two chords share and traces the ones that
  move; Practise rows are the teachers' drills (blocked, broken, 12 keys; each change, then the whole). Cite file and
  timestamp; say what no source gives (seventh-chord inversion fingering).
- **Name the result, not the mechanism (R42, v73):** "what bass only means": each hand shows the chord it plays
  (G + B♭maj7).
- **Transpose is one tap away and moves everything (R42, v73):** "transpose chords easier": the key in the staff's
  header opens half steps and twelve keys; every chord on the staff moves, spelled for the new key.
- **A floating group is centred as one (R42, v72):** "why this is looking so not centered": a button beside a
  centred button pulls the pair off centre; centre the group.
- **Hands play the whole chord; Split is the bass and the upper chord (R42, v71):** "when I select the left hand I
  want all the notes … both then … c e g and c e g in both hands … gm9 the g in the left hand and the BbMaj7 in the
  right". the learner's courses (Gorshkov) before the web.
- **Several chords, every action covers them (R42, v71):** the chord list is a checklist of what the staff shows;
  Play plays them all in the chosen mode.
- **A control shows what it does (R42, v70):** "be more creative and clear … ux ui best principles": R.H./L.H.
  chips and a 123 toggle were not understood; No abbreviation or bare icon where a word and the result fit.
- **A hand alone plays the chord, fingered (R42, v69):** "I want to play the c major chord in left hand with its
  fingerings and not c g c". A setting named for a hand plays the chord in that hand; reduce only past what a hand
  holds. A setting lives where its effect shows.
- **Adding a chord suggests the next one (R42, v69):** "you could improve it. be creative": the key's chords, likeliest
  first, voice-led.
- **Voicings checked against the owner's transcripts (R42, v68):** "check that our current are correct are match
  the researches": every voicing cites a transcript; Rootless on a dominant takes the 13th, not the 5th. Added
  Spread, So What, Kenny Barron, Upper structure, greyed where the chord does not take them. "leave out the alt".
- **Scales in every key with a key signature (R42, v68):** 15 per scale, spelled for the key, never D♯ major.
- **The hand setting changes the voicing, as pianists do (R42, v67):** "what relevance have the hands settings in
  … nineth chords and bigger … research the transcriptions". Research the owner's transcripts before the web.
- **Several chords on one staff (R42, v67):** "select more than one chord to be shown on the staff": names over the
  staff, + to add, × with Undo, All chords in order in the Play menu.
- **Play centred, alone, honey kept (R42, v63–v64):** "the play button is too appealing … centered", then "no
  revert the play button color": Play is one centred capsule in its honey tint.
  v66: "now the names get cut off": a bar label is never truncated; measure the longest name in the runtime.
- **Chords' settings live where they act (R42, v62):** Hand and Fingering in the Chord sheet after Voicing; the
  keyboard button at the header's right;.
- **No tab switch for the inversion; invent from the music (R42, v58):** "I dont like this tab switch, be more
  creative and think outside of the box". An inversion is a rotation, so it is a ring of the notes turned by tapping
  a note into the bass slot. Rejected for it so far: segmented track (v26, v57), ‹ › (v22), stepper pill (v52),
  pull-down (v56), ring (v58: "not understandable how it moves"). v59's moving stack: "it still moves too much". The
  inversion is a still choice of the bass note (v60): controls stay put, only the selection changes. v61, "better": each choice
  shows its result (a small staff of the chord it makes), and the slash in the name wears the bass note's colour.
  v62: the small staffs and the "Bass note" header rejected ("I dont like … remove them"); the plain row stays.
- **Choices one tap away, named by what they give (R42, v57):** "make the inventions easier to switch". A pull-down
  that hides a few related choices is two taps too many: show them side by side, each with its result (Root Cmaj7 ·
  1st Cmaj7/E). Notes are set as a theory table sets them (note over role), not dots with words. Research outside
  sites too (musictheorylessons.net), not only piano-data.
- **A heading is text, not chips and pills (R42, v56):** "I dont like inversions and the chips how they look". the notes a line of text with their roles; the ♭ in a chord
  name set as music. Never ‹ › for the inversion, never filled note chips.
- **No filled button inside a bar; groups of glass, one tinted primary (R42, v54):** "I dont like how this yellow
  button looks inside this toolbar. research the apple guidelines". Apple iOS 26: grouped glass items, the one
  primary action prominent in its own group, trailing.
- **Arrows sit beside the words they change; one action with a choice of how is one split button (R42, v52):** "the
  inversions can be interpreted as go to next chord … I'd put the play together with the arpeggiate". [▶ Play chord | ⌄] with Block chord · Arpeggio.
  v53: "the keyboard settings should be like separate from this play chord": the bar is the sound only.
- **The title is the button, without a ⌄ (R42, v51):** "remove the arrow on the big chord. it just works but without
  this arrow down".
- **Show the choices, don't hint at a gesture (R42, v46):** "the chord root switch looks interesting but you could
  improve it and make more clear". The chosen letter opens into its spellings (D♭ D D♯), each a tap; replaces
  tap-again cycling and its marks. **Rejected:** "no I don't like this". v47: one row, tap again, the chosen letter
  naming the next spelling. Never widen a segment into sub-buttons. v48: "this icon look like reset and is
  misleading": the chip reads "or C♯"; no turn arrow for a choice (it means reset or reload).
  v49: "now it can be interpreted as the c can be called c sharp": a pointer under the chosen letter reads "Tap
  again for C♯". Name a hidden gesture's action in words; a glyph or "or" is read as something else.
  v50: "is this a permanent or just a tooltip": a tip, gone for good once the gesture is used.
- **A chord sits where a chart puts it (R42, v45):** "why some chords like the f sharp are shown one octave lower".
  Root position starts in the octave from middle C for every root; no hidden register rule.
- **A chord root is spelled, a key is one of twelve (R42, v41):** "how can we though get chords like d flat or sharp
  or g flat or a sharp". The chord is spelled from the written root. v42: "some options are weird like c flat or e
  sharp". v43: "make just one row and when clicking multiple time on one note it will change": one row C to B;
  tapping the chosen letter again steps ♮ → ♯ → ♭, only the spellings a chart writes. Never extra rows for ♯ and ♭.
- **An extra note is a chord with a name, picked from the one list (R42, v39):** "I dont like this add a note how
  this looks, and is not understandable". No add-on chips beside the chord list, no avoid notes struck through; C7♭9
  is a row in Altered, as iReal Pro lists qualities.
- **A setting lives with the picture it changes; one label per key (R42, v38):** "I dont like these fingering
  settings … where can we put them". What a key reads is one choice (Synthesia's Key Labels: Names · Fingers ·
  None), in the Keyboard panel; the staff's finger numbers under Staff (Roland's notation settings); the hand that
  plays is its own row, not a fingering option.
- **A label on a key is one type step above its neighbour, not a headline (R42, v37):** "why the fingers numbers
  look so big". 14px on a white key over a 12px name.
- **A keyboard's settings are the Player's Keyboard panel, everywhere (R42, v36):** "I want like a piano settings like
  the player does. and in the header remain all other settings". The keyboard's button at the bar's end opens the
  tiles, Keys, and the piano's links; the gear keeps the page's own settings. Never mix the
  two in one sheet.
- **Fingering as printed charts set it (R42, v35):** "look at the examples where the fingering should be". Vita
  Piano: a number beside each note, right of the chord, the left hand on the bass staff; fingering changes with the
  inversion.
- **Test a canvas in its own runtime, not a look-alike (R42, v34):** "the settings arent working and the fingering
  still doesn't work". A local renderer showed both working; the canvas did not. Tap every control in
  `.scratch/r39/canvas/realrt.mjs` before saying it works.
- **A setting must earn its place; draw notation as engravers do (R42, v33):** "why do we need them … this setting
  is also weird". Piano gets the grand staff with no clef choice. A switch whose effect contradicts the page (a left-hand
  root under a slash chord) goes. - **Moved means removed from the old place too; a control lives on what it controls (R42, v32):** "you didn't
  remove the old settings". The keyboard's settings sit behind a keyboard button over the keys; a gear left with one
  group goes. Fingering
  belongs on the staff beside the notes, not in its own view.
- **A setting sits on what it changes; practice sits on the page (R42, v31):** "the staff settings are not very good
  … the practice in the player are too hidden".
  the ways to practise a chord are rows on its page. A bar holds one prominent action, trailing (Apple HIG).
- **The owner keeps some doubles (R42, v29):** "the chords choosing in both places was pretty good". Choosing the
  chord from its name and from the bar's first button stays; Ask before removing a double the owner liked.
- **Never: a practice page turned into a long builder of chip rows (R42, v27 undone):** "no, the previous layout
  directions were better". Chords keeps the keys, the chord, the views and a bar at the foot.
- **A page's bar holds its actions, not its settings (R42, v26):** "i dont like the bottom bar of the chords page …
  remove all the redundant things". What you choose sits on the thing
  it changes (the chord's name opens the chord;); what changes the
  display is behind the gear. One control per choice.
- **The app's tab bar is a symbol over a word (R42, v24):** "i don't like the bottom bar … of the app", after a
  words-only pill. Each tab the app's own icon over its name, the current place in a lens; a fade under the bar so
  content never shows below it. Corrected in v25 after reading the whole HIG page ("did you research the apple
  guidelines"): filled icons, and content peeks softly through the glass (scroll-edge blur), not a solid fade.
  Read a guideline in full before citing it.
- **Group by what a control changes (R42, v22):** "block chords or arpeggios change the player but they are in
  separate groups". A bar's capsules are subjects (the chord · its sound · the keyboard); a choice sits in the same
  capsule as the action it changes (Play beside Block or Arpeggio). Proximity, common region, similarity. A chord's
  inversion is written in its name as a slash chord (Cmaj7/E),.
- **Words beat symbols (R42, v21):** "make more clear", after a symbols-only bar. Every control shows a symbol and
  its current value in words (Block, Both hands), never a symbol alone except Play and Back.
- **A bar follows Apple's toolbar (R42, v20):** "apple guidelines provide more guides to toolbars". At most three
  groups, each a capsule with concentric items;
  text only where no symbol can say it (a chord's name); one tinted primary action. A switch of views is an
  equal-width segmented control of nouns, placed above what it switches.
- **Corners nest; a chosen tab contrasts (R42, v19):** "the inner corners doesn't match the roundness of the outer
  corners". An inner corner is the outer radius less the inset (a pill in a pill is a pill). The chosen item of a
  switch is filled in ink with white text, never a faint tint; tabs are named for what they show.
- **An icon alone is not enough (R42, v18):** "not all icons are very clear". A switch of views is words; a bar's
  buttons carry a caption under the icon; an icon draws what it does (stacked notes for a block chord, rising notes for
  an arpeggio). Only ‹ ›, Play and Back stand alone.
- **No setting twice on a page (R42, v17):** "we have now duplicate settings". Before publishing, list every control
  with its one home; a second control for the same choice goes (Chords: block or arpeggio one choice, the piano's
  settings only behind the Piano button).
- **A practice page can take the Player's shape (R42, v16):** "refactor the chords page from practice in the style of
  the songs player": Back · views · gear at the top, the views in the title's place (`TitleTabs`, owner 2026-11-13: "yes"), the keys edge to edge, the subject as the title, one floating bar
, sheets like the Player's. Spec `.scratch/r42/plan/chords.md`.
- **One canvas holds every place (v15):** "did you merge also the song player". The Player is a section of The ideal
  Piano Trainer, brought in whole with its R37 rulings, in the calm colours, opened by the song page's Continue and
  From the start; its Close goes back to the song. A place drawn elsewhere is merged, not left in its own canvas.
- **Calm is a language, not a layout (R41, drawn):** "i meant this minimalistic not that all the pages should look like
  it, refactor them properly and look at the example apps": the calm Chords page gives the tokens, the bare bar, grouped
  rows and the floating bar; each page keeps its own layout from its reference (iReal Pro's chart, Tenuto's answers,
  Soundbrenner's beats, Cleartune's needle, muted.io's circle, Flat's palette). Never one layout for every page.
  Spec `.scratch/r41/plan/songs-and-practice.md`.
- **Chords:** every chord type, fingering by hand and inversion, voicings to choose. Add, add2, add9 named as chords are.
- **Scales:** fingerings as the printed books give them, more kinds, Hanon 1–20, a tempo ladder.
- **Harmony:** draw the roles and moves on the chords (the Function · Root motion switch changed only letters).
- **Chord player:** Musicca's controls and options, with the best free sounds.
- **Metronome:** BPM large with −10 −1 +1 +10, a slider, Tap tempo, sound and meter, one Play; beats you set
  (accent, normal, silent), any meter, subdivisions, recorded sounds; one engine for the page, the Player and the quick
  metronome.
- **Practice ordered by relevance,** learning before tools, with a clear purpose; the Metronome's settings in place as
  Chordify's; the header's Tools a real menu, never a redirect; tools' settings never a single-option page or a redirect
  to app Settings; the Chord player simple, Apple-like, its drag and drop working (memory-palaces' fix); selection popups
  that say what is selected and why; the libraries editable like Songs, the built-in ones too; content kinds never
  mixed (patterns, method-book lessons, songs); Continue useful or gone; the sidebar like Claude's (2026-11-05).
- Grouped rows with a picture, a name and a line. **No strip hides options:** a key picker and menus.
- **Every Practice page has its own Settings page;** the app's Settings holds only what is app-wide.
- Every control says what it belongs to; "Root B" says of which scale; octaves are a stepper; formulas are computed; hands
  drawn as real hands; no half-empty top.

- **The libraries take Songs' row shape (loop, built to the Practice canvas):** Exercises, Accompaniment and
  Progressions show one search with Sort and filter (the sort first on a phone), place chips All · Favourites · Recently used · Mine,
  each book or style a heading with its count that folds, its rows in one card with hairlines: a 44px tile (a
  progression's opening, "I V", on its style's paint), the name bold over one line ("I – V – vi – IV"), the level, a ♡
  one tap; More only under a mouse. The chord chips and the description line left the rows; a progression's
  character is still found by the search. One `HeartToggle` for every library's ♡.
- **The overview takes the library's row shape (loop, built to the Practice canvas's Practice board):** each group a
  heading over one card of rows on a phone (`PlaceRow`: a 44px wash tile, the name bold over one short line, "Chord
  types, voicings, inversions"), large-tiled cards five across on a laptop; the bar's Tools (`ToolsButton`) beside
  Search on a phone. The books stay three rows (the library's places), not the canvas's merged Jazz vocabulary.
- **Loop round 18, the board's books as the app's (canvas 189):** the Practice board's Exercises are three rows, Finger technique · Barry Harris · Piano With Jonny, each with its count ("5 exercises"), as ruled; the merged Jazz vocabulary row is gone (2026-11-15).
- **The tiles in the board's paints (loop round 16, `r41_kit.py`'s `pic`):** the drills sage, the jazz books sky,
  the Chord player honey, and the four tools on the page's quiet fill (`plain`), the Tuner's needle in copper and
  the circle's C wedge in honey.
- **Accompaniment's rows in lilac (loop, 2026-11-15):** a pattern's row tile wears lilac, as the board's library draws it
  and as Accompaniment's own tile on Practice already did; it was yellow, one thing in two paints.
- **A pattern's keys show its chord (loop, 2026-11-15, `r63_pattern.py`):** a pattern's page marks C major's tones,
  C · E · G, in the chord-tone paints over its bar of C, as the board's keys do; a song tune stays unmarked. A key's
  name label ("C4") keeps one line.
- **A pattern's page as its board (loop, 2026-11-15, canvas 199):** its card holds the bar and one line of what it is;
  the longer description is The hands' ⓘ (owner: popups that show more with less UI). Used in is a small heading over
  one card of rows, as The hands, folded after five with Show all n; the big song cards went.
- **A library row's level in words (loop, 2026-11-15, the FingerTechnique board):** "Intermediate", as the Level filter
  names it, never pips and a number; on a phone it leads the row's line ("Advanced · Eight notes…") so a long Russian
  title keeps its width, in the laptop table its own column.
- **Recently used in the Practice libraries (loop, 2026-11-15, the Accompaniment and FingerTechnique boards):** the
  last eight rows opened, newest first, kept per library (`pt-practice-library` v3); a chip after Favourites and a
  place in the laptop's tree once anything was opened; at All the last three lead the page above Favourites. An
  exercise's, a pattern's or a progression's page marks it as it opens.
- **The Metronome's sound is a Woodblock (loop, 2026-11-15, the Metronome board):** the recorded block was named
  "Click"; it is named as the board and a drummer name it, "Вудблок" in Russian.
- **The Tuner at rest shows its A (loop, 2026-11-15):** before a note is heard, the reference note A₄ stands faint over
  "A4 = 440 Hz", where a grey dash read as a page still loading; the note heard replaces it in ink.
- **The Tuner without a microphone keeps its dial (loop 23, 2026-11-15, the Tuner board):** the A at rest and the
  needle stay; "The microphone is not available…" and Try again take the listening line's place, where the page had
  dropped to a line of text over nothing.
- **Keys named as the boards name them (loop, 2026-11-15):** where a page has no Labels setting (the Chord finder, a
  progression, a pattern) a chord's keys wear their paint and the note's name alone (`nameMark`), the degree left to
  the tiles and chips; and a page's keys grow to every key they light, never shrinking, so a chord found from A3
  keeps its root on the keyboard.
- **Scale settings is the board's one group of rows (loop 22, 2026-11-15, the Scales board's `SC_SET`):**
  Fingering a segmented row, then Start on, Compare with and Rhythm as rows with their value and a chevron, each
  opening its list; On the staff in its own group below. The labelled boxes it had were the old form, gone.
- **The Chord player's sheets in the same rows (loop 22, 2026-11-15, `r58_chordplayer`'s `CP_TAG`):** a section's
  sheet is one group (Section ›, Repeat with its stepper, Loop this section, Variation); its arranger sheet is the
  board's muted cards, Intro, Main each time through under its heading with the ⓘ, Ending, Into the next section.

- **The 3D piano plays (owner, 2026-11-15: "the 3d keyboard should also be interactive"):** on the Chords page and
  the Chord player, a press on the live 3D keys finds the key under the finger from the stage's own projection (a
  black key's top first, then the white key's), sounds it and holds it down in the pressed colour until the finger
  lifts, as the 2D keys do. The Player's 3D keys already sound on a press. One implementation in the shared stage
  (`piano_sheet.py`'s `HANDS3D`); the stage reports each key's corners (`pthands.bundle.js`, `__ptProj`).
- **Loop round 61, modal progressions (research 3.4):** the Modal style gains Dorian i–IV–v–i, Lydian I–II–vii–I and Phrygian i–♭II–III–i, each "… progression" (a Phrygian cadence is iv⁶–V); Mixolydian and Aeolian already had theirs; the walk-up is also named the Mario cadence (2026-11-17).

- **Intervals in the app as its board, at every width (port, 2026-11-17; craft-details, every width holds):** at 1024 the app still stood the interval in a 230 column beside a sticky column of Simple · Compound tabs and tiles, "Perfect fifth" wrapping, and kept the size line and the ⓘ's Up the rulings removed. It now reads as `r61_intervals.py`: the keys a band edge to edge, 28 over one centred column 880 wide at most (name and ⓘ, the music card, every interval in one grid of seven, 24 apart); no tabs, no size line, the ⓘ holding the meaning, Down and Inverts to. Scales' keys lost their laptop card the same way (one band, the `column` mode gone from the pinned keys).

- **The Chord player's two columns from 80rem (port, 2026-11-17; every width holds):** at 1024 the sheet's column was 270 wide beside the 420 one of the key's chords and styles: the first bar's name cut, the transport hanging over the sidebar's edge. As Settings and Songs, the side column joins at 80rem (`WIDE`); below it the sheet takes the width and the chords and styles are the dock's sheets, as on a phone.

- **The Streak calendar's card holds its month in its middle (v396; edge-lines, balanced insets):** its arrows stood 8 under the card's top and its last week 14 over its foot, so the month sat high; the foot is 4, the last row's cells 8 from the edge as the arrows are (Streak, StreakLaptop, and the app).

- **Focus leaves by the bar, never over the keys (v398; owner, 2026-11-17, on ChordPlayerLaptop: "the focus mode and the other features from the design. improve them"):** Exit focus was a capsule pinned at the window's foot, lying on the Chord player's keys and beside every floating transport. It is now the bar's first button while Focus is on, a 44 glass circle before Back (or the title, on a place with none), where the sidebar's own Focus button stood, its tip "Exit focus (Esc)"; Esc still leaves. Focus also hides the Songs library's pane, a side panel like any other, so the songs fill the window.

- **Focus in the app as on the canvas (port, v399):** the sidebar's head holds its toggle and Focus; Focus hides the sidebar and the side panels of content (the Chord player's chords and styles, Songs' library pane), never a page's own list of sections (Settings', which holds its Back and title); the screen and its docked keys and transport take the window; Exit focus is the bar's first button, Esc leaves; it lasts the session. The sidebar's name steps to 18 on one line so both head buttons fit.

- **A route state's title belongs to its line (v400, loop round 63; edge-lines, a heading nearest what it heads):** Page not found, This screen could not open and offline stood mark, title, line and ways on all 12 apart on the board and 10 in the app, so the title read as the mark's caption. The title is now 16 under the mark and 4 over its line, the ways on 20 below; the app also takes the board's 72 yellow tile, 18 title and 15 line where it had drifted to a grey circle, 22 and 16.

- **A chord run's music on the paper** (round 63, canvas v402): in the Player's chord run (`PlayChord`, `PlayChordLaptop`) the staff is the page's subject, so it leaves its lone card (edge-lines law 3); on a laptop it is set larger (zoom 1.35, 1018 wide with its progress bar) and centred in the band over the transport, where a 760px card had left 300px of bare paper; on a phone its cells widen to 84 so staff and progress bar share both edges.

- **A run's results, tidied** (round 63, canvas v403): the stat tiles carry the same three rows each (the figure with its unit quieter, one line of context, the label), 16 inset and 16 apart on a laptop, never a label pushed to the tile's foot by a stretch; Timing reads "9 on time · 2 early · 1 late", Tempo "72 BPM · In Wait mode"; The run stands on the paper under its heading, on the column's edge, as in the run itself. `PlayChord`, `PracticeResult` and their laptops.

- **Spacing on the scale across Practice** (round 63, canvas v406): the Practice laptop cards drop their empty third line, so their insets balance (16 and 16, not 16 and 28); every chip row's pills are 36 in a 44 target (4px rings, -4 margins), so the boxes sit 12 from the search and the heading and 16 apart to the eye, never 11; a library's fold heading (Songs, Progressions, Accompaniment) is pulled in by 4, sitting 4 over its card, never 3; the Chord player's sections stand 16 apart, never 18; on Intervals the interval's name sits 8 over the card it names and 24 under the keys, never 16 from both (a heading as far from what it heads as from what is above, edge-lines). Scales keeps 16 under its name, which is two picker pills, not a heading.

The keyboard grows only for a key outside its range, a range that fits the view opens edge to edge, and Scales' keys are the run's lit octave, not the left hand's too.

Where the music reaches past the phone's range (a progression's upper chords, a pattern's two hands) the keys grow and scroll, never cutting a lit key.

- **Chords' 3D keys 8 under the bar on the board too (2026-11-19):** the board kept a 56 spacer of its own after the bar, which the float pass already spaces, so the keys stood 62 under the bar over a bare band; the spacer is gone (phone and laptop), as the app draws it (no control reserves space it does not use).

- **The Chord player exports, opens on the last sheet by its name, and keeps up with a long song (owner, 2026-11-21: "make me export, and that it opens in the last opened and improve performance"):** its ··· gains Export MIDI (the band once through as a Standard MIDI File: the meter and tempo's track, then Piano, Bass on General MIDI's bass program, Drums on channel 10, a part turned off in Settings left out, the piano's swing written in; named "Song (130 BPM).mid") and Export chart (the chart as text: title, key · time · tempo, each section under its name with ×n, four bars to a line between bar lines, `B♭:2` for a chord shorter than its bar). A song or a progression put in hand keeps its name (the store's `scratchName`): the bar, the recent sheets' menu and Save as name it, and the Chord player reopens on it from Practice or a reload, where it read "Untitled chord sheet". The chart is drawn as memoised sections and boxes held by stable acts, a section's key and sections shared from one reading of the text to the next, and the drag sensors held for good: on the 112-bar Isus s-a născut, six seconds of playing went from 11 long tasks (931 ms, the longest 195) to one (69 ms), and an added chord's longest task from 129 to 84 ms. Every download's name passes one `fileTitle`.
- **The Chord player imports a chart (owner, 2026-11-21: "and import"):** its ··· gains Import chart…, before the exports: a text file of the chart as Export chart writes it (or as typed by hand: a title, `F · 4/4 · 130 BPM`, section names in English or Russian with ×n, bars between bar lines), or the app's one-line form, is put in hand under its title (else the file's name) at its tempo, with Undo; a file with no chords says "No chords could be read from junk.txt" and changes nothing. Export then Import gives back the same chords, tempo and name (checked on the 112-bar Isus s-a născut).

## Sounds

The best free ones: CC0 (VCSL) for the metronome and kit, Salamander (CC-BY, credit in About) for the piano. Musicca's files
are reference only, but progressions are facts.

## PWA and touch

- **It feels like a real app:** no browser bubbles, loupe or callouts when installed.
- **The keyboard** swipes while a key is held, so no mini-map; it folds the same everywhere.
- **Fields stay above the phone's keyboard** as memory-palaces does it: field, label and buttons in sight, nothing jumping.
- **The Update button must work:** checked with two real builds.
- **Show what the device has:** Computer keys only on a computer.

## Navigation and bars
- **Every laptop window stands centred, one size, as claude.ai's Settings (owner, 2026-11-20: "place the dialogs correctly and have you researched the claude ai ui flow"):** claude.ai's Settings (saasui.design's screens of it, 2026) is a window 922 by 720 centred both ways on a 1729×1081 screen, its nav on the left and × at the top right, the same height on every page; its small dialogs are centred too. Ours stood two ways: the app's Settings nearly the whole window (1200 wide, the window less 96 high) and a thing's Settings 48 under the top, as tall as its page. Now both are centred on the window and one height, `--window-height` (720, less on a short screen): the app's Settings 960 wide, a thing's 720; a page turned inside never moves or resizes the window. The confirmations and New sheets were centred already. Canvas: SETDLG and THINGWIN.
- **A card's edge is never cut (owner, 2026-11-20: "why all the cards in the app are clipped"):** a card's 1px ring (`.card`, `ring-1`) is drawn outside its box, and two kinds of scroller cut it: every folding section (`FoldBody`) slid its body inside an `overflow-hidden` wrapper flush with the cards, so each card in a fold lost its top and bottom line; and a horizontal shelf (`overflow-x-auto`, which makes the other axis clip too) sat flush above its covers. `FoldBody` now gives what it slides 4 of room on each side (only while open, so a folded body still closes to nothing), and the shelves (All songs, Explore, the courses' covers) 4 above. A detector (`ring.mjs` in the session's scratchpad) found no other cut ring across Learn, Songs, Practice and Profile at 402 and 1710.
- **A screen that will not open says which way it failed, as the AppLayers board (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-20):** the app titled both route states "Something went wrong", offline or not, with Reload under each; the board draws two: "This screen could not open" over "Reloading usually fixes it. The error has been sent so it can be fixed." with Back and Reload, and "You’re offline" over "This screen opens once you’re back online. Your songs and lessons on this device still open." with Back and Try again. The app now says each as the board does. A draft's failed publish, restore or discard no longer says "Something went wrong" either (ux-writing: no generic error): "It didn’t go through. Try again."
- **The app's layers as the AppLayers board, measured (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-20):** the welcome is the board's 354 box (it was 362), 20 in on every side (24 above), its title 24 (22), Language 12 over its choice (8); the deletion gate's box the same, Sign out and Keep account side by side, Keep account the one honey act (it was ink); the update band 52 tall (56) with the card's 14 corners, the out-of-room notice 16 in, 12 at its foot, its parts 8 apart and the menu's 16 corners; on a laptop the notices stand centred at the foot, 460 wide (they stood at the right, 440). A screen that cannot open (Page not found, Something went wrong, offline) keeps the place's Search in its bar and the tab bar on a phone, as the board draws it (it had neither, and a folder that was gone left no way out but its one button); its mark is the board's icon (a circled ?, a circled !, no Wi-Fi) where it drew a bold ? or !, centred between the bar and the tab bar, its ways on 50 tall. The board's states now open over the bare page, not over Search, and its notice stands under the 62 status bar (it overlapped it by 4).
- **The search palette as its board, measured (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-19):** a suggestion row is 60 as its code says (the board measured 76: its min-height padded without border-box), the headings and chips stand on the 16 inset (they stood 4 and 14), Recent searches carries Clear at its heading's end as the board draws it, and every sheet's Done stands 4 in from its 16 edge as `BSHEET`'s (it stood 8 in).
- **A laptop page's bar ends 28 from the window's edge (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-19):** the kit's `LPAD` pads a page's 56 bar 40 at its leading edge and 28 at its trailing one; the app's bar padded 44 on both, so every laptop page's last bar button (the gear, Piano, ···) stood 16 further in than its board. `ScreenHeader` now pads 28 at the trailing edge from a laptop's width.
- **A large title after Back stands 12 off it on a phone, 8 on a laptop (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-19):** `TITLEBAR` and `LTITLEBAR`; the app's stood 6 off it (Progressions, Accompaniment, Finger technique, Courses, a level).
- **The laptop's three heads as the boards' (loop, the board-to-app port, 2026-11-19):** measured against Chords, Scales, the Metronome, Courses, a level, Your lessons, Practice and Profile, every laptop bar with Back stood 16 low and 12 in too far (Back 52 in and 22 down where the boards put it 40 in and 6 down, so the keys began at 72, not 56). The boards draw three heads, and `ScreenHeader` now takes each: a page's 56 bar flush with the window's top, Back 40 in (`LPAD`); a large title after Back in an 88 head, Back 44 in and 22 down (`LTITLEBAR`: Courses, a level, Your lessons); a place's 88 head, its title on the 56 column (Practice, Learn, Profile). - **The laptop sidebar and a place's title as the boards (loop, the board-to-app port by text, 2026-11-19):** the sidebar was 256 wide with its places 12 in, the brand 22px and the places in the caption's soft ink; it is the boards' 248 (`lg:w-62`, `pl-sidebar` with it), its places 16 in, the brand 24, a place in `--nav-foreground` (#4A433C) and the current one in `--nav-current-foreground` (#1A1612), each with its Night value; a place's large title is 30 on a laptop (28 on a phone), as every laptop board's. Built-in rows keep their heart.
- **Show all under Add member (owner, 2026-11-16: "why the show all is not under the add member, do they need to be together"):** a group's Members card ends with Add member, its last row; "Show all 14 ›" is a quiet link under the card, not a row inside it. Admin's group page, phone and laptop.
- **Examples first, by file name and on the web (owner, 2026-11-16):** before refactoring a control, component or page, its examples in `piano-data/reference-apps` are found by file name and opened, and screenshots of it in shipped apps are searched on the web. Dorico and Flat looked at most; `piano-chords-and-scales-app` welcome; `music-theory-book-app` for learning content only, never UI. Piano Trainer's own old screenshots were removed from `piano-data` ("remove the all examples of the piano-trainer app").
- **One focus per page; what floats is centred on it (owner, 2026-11-16):** "the floating controls like in the scales page should be centerd by the main focus content … the practices on the right robs the focus from the main content … all of the main content should be correctly focudsed". On a laptop the main content is one centred column that leads; what follows from it (Practise in the Player, the key's rows) reads after it in that column, not in a rival column beside it; the floating bar is centred on that column (`ui-and-mockups.md`, Layout).
- **Scales and keys on a laptop as one centred column (loop round 58):** the run, the scale's chords, the key's rows and Practise in the Player read down one 880 column under the full-width keys; Practise no longer stands as a right column, and Tempo · Play scale · Hands is centred on the column (2026-11-16).
- **Every Practice page on a laptop as one centred column (loop round 58):** Intervals, a progression, a pattern, an exercise and the Chord finder had their own content at the left and a rival column at the right (the interval grid, the walks, the hands and Used in, the exercise's choices, Also read as), with the floating bar centred between them; each now reads down one 880 column under the keys, its subject first and the rest after it, the bar centred on the column (2026-11-16).
- **Scales draws the key on the circle of fifths, not in words (loop round 58; owner, 2026-11-16: "what is with this relative minor, refactor htis. and on the circle of fifths. this doenst make any sense"):** the rows "Relative minor · E minor" and "On the circle of fifths · Between C and D" are now the key's slice of the circle, IV · I · V, each major over its relative minor with its signature, the scale's own cell lit and each a tap to that key; the heading opens the Circle of fifths, the one home of the whole circle.
- **What a page says twice goes (loop round 58; owner: "duplicate content"):** a pattern's "The hands" rows (the line under its staff already says what each hand does), the progression's sentence under its staff that restated its tiles, and the exercise's staff caption that repeated How to play.
- **The Chords staff says each thing once (loop round 58; owner: "duplicate content"):** the letters beside its noteheads (B G E C) repeated the keys' tags above, and "Right hand 1 2 3 5" under it repeated the fingers printed beside the notes; both went. The chord symbol over the staff stays: it is the button that changes that chord once the staff holds several.

- **Question every part before polishing it (owner, 2026-11-16):** "you shouldnt just take the current app design as so it should be … question every decision and every component … so that we dont have unneded components unneded pages, duplicate content … are the tabs correct? do we need them … what is already know by the user". The drawing today is a draft: each page, tab, section, control and explanation is asked whether it is needed, said elsewhere, better merged, moved or shown as a picture, or already known; what fails is removed or merged in the same turn, and only what survives is polished (`ui-and-mockups.md`, Building a screen, step 2; `SKILL.md`, Loops and refactors). The same is asked between pages and across the app (owner: "you should also check between the pages for these thigns and across the app"): two pages showing the same thing, a page repeating a section of another, a tab or page that could be its parent's section, one act reachable two ways; one home is kept, the rest link to it or go.
- **An empty list offers the other way in, as a link (loop round 57, from Flat's empty playlist, `flat-score-editor/096`):** an empty library or folder shows its picture in a rounded square, a short title, one true line and one link to the next step that is not the + (Find a song in Explore; Add songs from your library); the floating + stays the one way to create, so no honey button repeats it. Select hides while there is nothing to select.
- **One empty-state picture, Flat's (loop round 57):** every empty, no-results, offline, error and no-microphone state wears its mark in a 72 honey rounded square (Flat's `096`), where six drew a 64 grey circle and Songs a square. The bar keeps the centred title at every width.

- **Replaced 2026-11-18 by craft-details' "Bars stay, quietly" (the owner's articles, 2026-11-17: a bar is never hidden by scrolling); the canvas no longer hides it. Was: on a phone the floating bar steps aside while reading (owner, 2026-11-15, the adaptable UI):** scrolling down a
  page past its first 56px slides the floating bar up and out (220ms, the app's one curve); the first move up brings
  it back. Apple HIG, Toolbars (hide bars on swipe so the content has the screen) and Material 3's top app bar (hide
  on scroll, return on scroll up). Under reduced motion it changes without moving. Every phone board with the floating
  bar (59), by `index.py`; the floating actions at the foot stay.

## Actions and menus
- **One laptop toast, where the ruling put it (loop round 54's one toast, measured again 2026-11-18):** the laptop's toast stood in five places: centred 460 and 28 up on 13 boards (the ruling), centred 420 and 32 up in the practice libraries, 420 at the right 40 in on Learn, Courses, Your lessons and their settings, 420 at the right of a lesson and a course, the update banner 440 at the right 28 in. Every one is now centred on its pane, 460 wide, 28 up, and over a lesson's floating foot bar 116 up, clear of it by 15 (consistency; Material 3, snackbars).

- **Every confirm as the board's ASK (loop 24):** 372 wide on the page's paper, 20 corners, 20 in; the question 18
  bold sans at the start, its line 15 on 1.45; Cancel (sand) and the act as two equal 44 pills, a loss filled in
  crimson; no band under them. One primitive, so ConfirmDialog, the deletion gate and every ask follow
  (2026-11-15).

- **A New sheet opens with its name focused (loop round 18):** New song, a New folder, a New group and every `NewSheet` put the caret in their name field as they open, the phone's keyboard up, as iOS's New Folder does; the learner types at once instead of tapping the field first.

- **One way to each action:** "why are there duplicate buttons and actions".
- **Drag, never Move up or down:** "why dont drag and drop".
- **Short menu items.** **Change it everywhere:** "and not just in one place".
- **Floating buttons:** the main action a honey 56px circle, a second a raised 48px; a dial opens as a card of rows.
- **Cards whole:** "don't you see how bad this looks with these cut borders".
- **Undo instead of asking** (2026-11-07, "adopt also this"): a loss that can be undone happens at once with Undo in its
  toast; only what can't be undone, or reaches other people, asks first. Replaces "confirm before losing or hiding work".
- **A loss is shown as a loss** (2026-11-07, "i don't like your rejection", of leaving out loss framing): name what goes,
  by name or count, and let the way out name its cost; always true, never a fake countdown.
- **Filter chips show their counts** ("Advanced 6"); a chip with none is dimmed, not hidden.
- **Progress, by best practice** (2026-11-07, "use the best practices"): one practice record (time this week against
  last week, one bar per day on a value axis), a streak with milestones and rest days earned by practising (one per 7
  days, two at most), and the week shared in a group. Drawn, not built.
- **Only what works here:** "only the relevant actions and ui should be shown and not ui that doesn't work". A choice never
  moves you to another tab.
- **A menu leaves out what cannot be done** (loop, 2026-11-13, from "only the relevant actions"): no greyed item with a
  reason in a menu or a gear (Feedback before a take, Voice commands without a voice, Sections with one, Hide on the
  last staff in sight); an in-place control may still grey.
- **One icon, one meaning.** **One home per idea:** the page shows what you look at, its Settings how it always behaves.

- **In Edit, a row's Remove is a red minus, not a red word (loop, 2026-11-15):** the Courses page and a course in
  Edit drew "Remove" in red on every row, a column of red words that squeezed the titles to three lines. Each is now
  the iOS delete control, a 22px red circle with a white minus in a 44px target beside the drag handle, named
  "Remove <title>" with its tooltip (Apple HIG, Lists and tables: editing). Undo still follows in the toast.

- **One menu shell on every board (2026-11-17, every `role="menu"` on the canvas grouped by its look):** seven shapes stood for one kind of thing (corners 14, 16 and 18; insets 6 and 8; four shadows; solid, glass and the page's own colour). Every menu now wears the kit's `MENUSHELL`: 6 in, 16 corners, its 10-round rows concentric, the glass with its ring: the rows' ···, Sort, New, Play as, Play over, What it plays, Hands, the Player's View and Song, and the laptop's account menu. Chords' own spacing snap had turned the 6 into 8 (a drifted copy in one area's post-process); menus are now exempt from it.
- **The account menu says only what is true (2026-11-17):** its Help opened Legal, a link that did not go where it said; the User guide's home is Settings › Support, so Help leaves the menu (Profile, Settings, Language, Sign out stay).
- **One dialog shape and one popover shape on every board (2026-11-17, every `role="dialog"` grouped by its look):** centred dialogs stood at 20 and 22 corners, ten without a ring or shadow, and the laptop dialogs made from a phone sheet kept the sheet's grabber inset (6 at the top) and its home-indicator foot (40 at the bottom), so their content sat off centre (`edge-lines`: an unbalanced box). Now every centred dialog is the sheet's 22 all round with the kit's ring and shadow and 20 inside; one helper, `LAPSHEET` (r53), makes a laptop dialog from a phone sheet where three copies did. Popovers at 14 and 18 (a lesson's The colours, Scales' Tempo) are the menu shell's 16; Search's palette is 22 (similarity: one shape per kind of layer).
- **Every form on a laptop is the centred dialog (2026-11-17, every laptop layer named New or Rename listed by kind):** New song, New folder and New group opened centred, while New course, New lesson and Rename opened as popovers pinned to odd corners (Rename's left edge at the window's middle). A form is the one thing a laptop centres (`ui-and-mockups`), so all seven now go through `LAPSHEET`, the one helper (moved to `r41_lib`). New course's captions stood 12 from the chips above and 12 from their own (`edge-lines`: a heading as far from what it heads as from what is above reads as the last group's end); `LABELLED` keeps a caption 8 from its control, so the sheet's 16 falls between fields (proximity).
- **Every popup's captions held to their controls (2026-11-17, the popup walker `checks/layers.mjs` now fails a caption as near the group above as its own, and a centred dialog whose top and bottom insets differ):** Sort and filter's Sort by and each filter's name, and Join with a link's Invite link, stood 12 or 16 from both neighbours; each now goes through `LABELLED`, 8 from its control (proximity).
- **The whole popup walk (2026-11-17, 257 popups on 129 boards opened):** captions floating between groups in the Chord player's settings (Instruments, Playing, Music), a section's Main, each time through, a chord's Suggested chords, New course's Cover and Rename's Name now sit 8 over what they name (proximity). Kept: titled menus (Play as, Play over, What it plays), whose caption carries its own margin; the Chords pickers' OUTSIDE, an artefact of opening on a fresh board.
- **Every icon-only control names itself on hover (2026-11-17, every empty `<button>` on 129 boards read; `scan-first` 8):** the library's floating + had no tooltip on six boards (Songs, All songs, a folder), so the kit's dial now carries New; Chords' Back carried its tip twice, and its own tip pass now leaves a control that already has one. The full lines sweep (110 boards) found nothing new beyond cases already kept (Check's centred prompt, Sign in's lone Close, the Play boards' Start under their Setup sheet); the lines tool now measures an open dialog alone, not against the page behind it.
- **One list row on every board (2026-11-17, every two-line row on 110 boards measured):** 193 rows stood at a 16 title over a 14 line; Archive's and Trash's songs, Chords' ways to practise and Profile's practice history had a 13 line, and Songs' playlists a 17 title. They are now the same row (similarity: alike things look alike). Kept: a level's current lesson in 700, its emphasis.
- **Legal's section headings at the group heading's 20 (2026-11-17, every `h2` on the canvas grouped by size):** they stood at 19 beside 20 everywhere else; the other sizes follow their roles (36 a subject's name, 22 a lesson's section, 20 a group).
- **The Settings dialog on a laptop wears the dialog's 22 (2026-11-17, every card and surface grouped by corner):** it stood at 16 while every centred dialog is 22; its deeper shadow stays, as the largest window. Surfaces otherwise hold: cards 14, fields and controls 12.
- **The laptop Player's views wear the Play · Edit switch's shape (2026-11-17, similarity: one control, one look):** the four views (Sheet, Diagrams, Grid, Lyrics) sat 34 high in a 4px tray, each 112 wide, beside Play · Edit at the bar's full 44 hugging its word: two segmented controls in one bar, two shapes. The views now take the kit's `MODESW` values: 44 high, hugging the word, 15 on 600 in ink, off 500 in soft ink (`r75_player_views.py`).
- **Every sheet, dialog and the search palette is `aria-modal` (2026-11-18, WCAG 4.1.2; the lines' scrim rule):** a modal layer says so, so a screen reader stays inside it and `lines.mjs` measures only the open layer, never the page behind its scrim (the search palette's lines had been drawn over the Learn page under it). The tool's card pass now keeps to the open layer too.
- **On a laptop, Profile's place shows in the sidebar (2026-11-18, HIG Sidebars: the selected item says where you are):** when the account row became Claude's menu button it lost the current wash, so Profile and a run's results (reached from Profile) showed nothing current. On those pages the account row now wears the current wash and `aria-current`, as Learn, Songs and Practice do on theirs (`lapwin.py`). The same holds for every page the account menu opens: Settings (over Profile), Administration and Legal.
- **Signed out, the sidebar says so (2026-11-18, truthful status; ux-writing, Trust):** Sign in's laptop board showed "Ana Popescu" in the account row and Sign out in its menu while no one was signed in. Signed out, the row is a person's outline and Sign in, current on that page; its menu reads Not signed in, Settings, Language, then Sign in apart.
- **A song list on a phone is one list, not a card per song (2026-11-18, edge-lines' laws: "rows of Explore: a list, not a card each"; NN/g Cards; M3 "on a phone consider swapping cards for lists"):** All songs, a playlist, a shared playlist, search results and Explore drew every song as its own ringed card 8–12 apart, a wall of boxes for one homogeneous list, while the laptop already showed them as one table. Each is now one card holding its rows, a hairline from the title's edge (84: 12, the 56 tile, 16), moving right with the title in Select. Explore's rows take the same 56 tile and 84 edge (they were 64 and 84 by another sum). Supersedes loop 61's "each song its own card" (`SONGROW`, `SONGLIST`, `FEEDROW`).
- **A shared playlist is All songs' table on a laptop, and its songs' menu is a viewer's (2026-11-18, one shape per sibling page; no misleading acts):** Choir hymns drew two columns of rows where every other playlist is the table; it is now the table. Its song menu offered Edit score, Settings and Archive on someone else's song; View only, it holds Practise, Add to a playlist and Favourite (`VIEWITEMS`).
- **An empty list has one shape, and a new learner's sidebar is empty too (2026-11-18, similarity; one clear next act, `craft-details` Empty):** Songs' first run and an empty playlist wore a 20px title, one of them in a 420-tall card; both are now the kit's one empty state (r53's `EMPTY`: a 72 honey tile, an 18/700 title, a 15 line, at most one link, no box), as Archive, Trash and Notifications. On a laptop the first run hides the sidebar's counts and every playlist but Favourites, so the sidebar no longer promises 93 songs beside "0 songs". On a phone the first run's Explore link went: the Explore shelf right below it is the way there (one way to each act).
- **The kit's gallery shows the kit as it is (2026-11-18, similarity; no misleading samples):** Admin › Component gallery's Empty state was a boxed grey line no screen uses; it is now the one empty state (72 honey tile, 18/700 title, 15 line, no box). Its Actions row showed Secondary and Quiet, the same button twice; Secondary went, and the button kind nothing used (`ink`) left the kit. - **Search's results show what they are, and the sheet holds still (loop round 42; scan-first 3):** the suggestions wore pictures but the typed results were words alone; each result now carries the same 36px picture the thing wears elsewhere (a scale's run, a chord's keys, a progression's numerals, a song's incipit, a folder's stack, a lesson's staff). The phone's Search sheet stood at its content's height, so it dropped from full to a third as results ran out; it now stays at the large detent (86%) whatever is typed, as iOS's search sheets do.
- **The laptop palette holds still:** the palette grew and shrank with what was typed, and its keys scrolled away under a long list. It now stands at one height (600), its field pinned at the top and its keys at the foot on a hairline, the results scrolling between, as Raycast and Linear's palettes do.
- **A course card names its level, not its shelf (loop round 43; craft-details: a badge is one or two words):** each card on Learn's Courses strip was badged with its shelf's name, "Beginner courses", a plural on one course and the same words four times over. The badge is now the level alone (Beginner, Intermediate); the Courses page keeps the shelf headings, where the plural is right.
- **Running text at most 65ch on a laptop (loop round 43; craft-details, Text):** on the wide laptop columns a lesson's paragraphs, a level's description and a pattern's or progression's note ran 85 to 120 characters a line. Each now stops at 65ch; the figures beside them (the keys, the staff) keep the column's full width.
- **A table's headers stand over their columns (loop round 43; scan-first 1, edges):** on My songs and a folder on a laptop the header row kept 12px of right padding the rows did not, so its flex columns came out narrower and Composer and key stood 5px, Progress 10px, off the values under them. The header now shares the rows' edges; measured, each header starts on its column's first letter.
- **A song's chords in one row on a laptop:** the laptop's chord cards wrapped, five on a row and Em alone below. They are now a six-column grid across the column, one row for the song's chords.
- **Explore's covers fill the shelf on a laptop:** the six-column shelf held 150px covers in wider cells, so each row stopped 33px short of its See all and the gaps were uneven to the eye. A cover now fills its cell, square, and the shelf runs edge to edge under its heading.
- **Each book of exercises its own picture (loop round 43; ux-principles, one thing one icon):** Barry Harris and Piano With Jonny both wore the swung-eighths tile, two books under one picture. Barry Harris now wears his 6th-diminished scale, "6°" on lilac; the swung eighths stay with Piano With Jonny.
- **Chords wears the one bar; a group on a page is the white card (loop round 43; one bar shape, DESIGN.md's groups):** a sweep of every laptop board's bar found Chords alone drawing its own (68 tall on a laptop with an 18px title set left; 16px on the phone) where every other page uses the kit's TOP, 56 tall, its title centred. Chords now uses TOP on both sizes, its 3D stage starting under the bar. The Metronome's settings sat in a grey filled group on the page, where groups on a page are the white card with a hairline ring (the fill belongs to groups inside a sheet); it is the white card now, and so are the Tuner's Reference pitch and the Chord finder's Also read as · Played · In these keys, the last two a sweep of every board found.
- **Chords keeps the app's sidebar on a laptop:** Chords was the one Theory page drawn full-window on a laptop, with no sidebar, where Scales, Intervals and the tools keep it. It now has the same sidebar, drawn by one helper (`SIDEBAR` in `calm_kit.py`) that every laptop board uses, put in after the page's own type pass so it is the same drawing.
- **Fingering in ink on every staff (loop round 43; one look per thing):** the Scales staff printed its fingering in a purple found nowhere else in the palette, where the Chords staff prints it in ink, as engravers do. Scales' fingering is ink now.
